## Architect-ruled contract â€” S3 cohort v4

The reviewed contract is deterministic and fail closed:

1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is â‰¤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) Â± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

    @staticmethod
    def _eligibility(blockers, errors, reads, jobs) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not blockers:
            return {"status": "CURRENT", "stage": None, "reasons": [], "errors": []}
        reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(code for _, code in blockers))
        stage = min((st for st, _ in blockers), key=_ORDER.index)
        has_result = all(r.get("selection") for r in reads.values())
        if has_result:
            status = "STALE_PROJECTION"
        elif any(r["status"] == "FAILED" for r in reads.values()):
            status = "FAILED"
        elif all(r["status"] == "UNASSESSED" for r in reads.values()) and not jobs:
            status = "UNASSESSED"
        else:
            status = "PENDING"
        return {"status": status, "stage": stage, "reasons": reasons, "errors": errors}

# Backend testing guide

How to run the backend suite, when to run all of it, and how to prove a full
run was real. Database setup itself is in [README-db.md](README-db.md).

## When to run what

- **While building a phase:** run the focused files for the area you changed,
  plus adjacent integration tests. When an interface crosses into the host or
  gateway, also run those suites.
- **At the end of an ordinary slice:** one real-Postgres full backend suite,
  on the slice's final revision, with the JUnit zero-skip check below. Cite it
  in the review packet.
- **Reviewers** run the focused files relevant to their findings and cite the
  implementer's same-revision full evidence. A reviewer reruns the full suite
  only for a finding that needs it.
- **One full suite at a time on this machine.** Concurrent full runs were not
  attempted and are not proven safe; a full run is disk-bound, so a second one
  (or another Postgres-heavy agent) slows both and makes timing meaningless.
- OPS-01's baseline and comparison runs are the one documented exception to
  "one full suite per slice".
- `tools/check-all.ps1` is a separate required guard command; it does not run
  pytest, and pytest does not replace it.

## The test database

`ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` names a base database whose name ends in `_test`
(Compose creates `assetops_test`; agents may use a private one such as
`assetops_<task>_test`). Without it every Postgres module is reported as
**skipped**, and such a run is not valid full-suite proof.

The base database is used only as an administrative connection. At
configuration time `tests/conftest.py` calls `pg_harness.route()`, which
rewrites `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` to a run-owned name before any test module
is imported:

```
<base without _test>_pt<run>_<tag>_test     tag = main (serial), gw0, gw1... (xdist workers), tpl (template)
```

On first use the harness migrates the template to head once, then every
Postgres module's `engine` fixture drops the run's worker database `WITH
(FORCE)` and clones it from the template (`CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE`), and
asserts the clone is at the Alembic head. Each module therefore still starts on
a fresh, fully migrated schema; migration up/downgrade tests still run in place
on that module's clone. `store` fixtures yield and dispose their engine.

Between tests the S1 `store` fixture empties the same tables
`TRUNCATE foundation_revision, source, gateway, asset, site_import, site, tenant
CASCADE` would, but with `DELETE` under `session_replication_role = replica`
(`pg_harness.empty_tables`). TRUNCATE rebuilds every index and on PostgreSQL 16
each rebuild forces a data-file sync, which Docker Desktop makes slow and
erratic (measured 1.4 s quiet, 22-25 s under disk pressure, per test; DELETE
0.14 s). `test_pg_harness.py` proves the DELETE set equals the tables TRUNCATE
CASCADE reports. `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLEANUP=truncate` restores TRUNCATE.

- In parallel (`-n 2`) the xdist controller runs no tests. It makes the run
  id and passes it to each worker; each worker routes itself to
  `..._pt<run>_gw<N>_test` before it imports a test module. The first worker
  that needs the template builds it under an advisory lock and the others reuse
  it. Workers drop only their own database; the controller drops the template
  and anything else the run left (a crashed worker's database) at the end.
  Under `--dist loadfile` every module stays on one worker, so module-scoped
  fixtures behave as serially. With `--no-loadscope-reorder` modules are handed
  out in collection order, and workers move the two long S4 Finding modules
  (`conftest.START_FIRST`) to the front so they start first; serial order is
  unchanged.
- Parametrize IDs must not be random: xdist refuses to run when workers collect
  different node IDs (`Different tests were collected between gw0 and gw1`).
- Nothing is provisioned for `--collect-only`, for a selection without Postgres
  modules, or with the URL unset.
- At the end of the session the run drops its own databases. A killed run's
  databases are swept by the next run with the same base: it drops
  `<base>_pt*` databases only when their run holds no live session (each run
  keeps one lease session whose `application_name` is
  `assetops-test-run:<run>`).
- `ASSETOPS_TEST_TEMPLATE=0` turns routing off and restores the old behaviour
  (drop `public` and migrate in place per module). Use it only if cloning is
  unavailable; it is serial-only and slower.
- Measurement knobs, off by default: `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLONE_STRATEGY`
  (`WAL_LOG` or `FILE_COPY`) and `ASSETOPS_TEST_SYNCHRONOUS_COMMIT` (applied with
  `ALTER DATABASE` to run-owned databases only). Server-wide settings are never
  changed by the tests.

## Commands (PowerShell, from the repository root)

```powershell
$env:ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql+psycopg://assetops:assetops-local-dev@127.0.0.1:55432/assetops_test"
$py = ".venv\Scripts\python.exe"

# S1 Foundation and bindings
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s1_foundation_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_cutover.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings.py

# S2 ingestion and evidence
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s2_evidence_ledger_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_gateway_access_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_site_evidence_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_registered_basis_postgres.py

# S3 dispatch
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_demo_configuration.py

# S4 Finding and money
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_evidence_history_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_assumptions_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_recompute_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_consequence_work_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_drain_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_power_demo_case_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_tick_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_health_postgres.py

# Complete suite - the normal full command (two workers), with JUnit for the zero-skip proof
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml

# Complete suite, serial (fallback; also what a serial re-check of one file uses)
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml
```

### Before a long full run

Start the keep-awake helper in a separate window (or as a background job). It
asks Windows not to idle-sleep while any pytest process runs, changes no power
settings, and exits after 20 idle minutes. A machine that sleeps mid-run turns
a 40-minute suite into hours (seen in OPS-01: one run reached 34% in 4 h).

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\keep-awake-during-tests.ps1
```

Cap every full run at twice its expected wall time (see Timing below). A run
past the cap is reported as a hang, with the node ID it is stuck on (from
`pg_stat_activity` and the pytest log), not waited on.

### Zero-skip proof (from JUnit, not the summary line)

```powershell
[xml]$j = Get-Content .pytest-junit.xml
$cases = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase")
$skipped = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[skipped]")
$failed = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[failure or error]")
"cases=$($cases.Count) skipped=$($skipped.Count) failed=$($failed.Count)"
$skipped | ForEach-Object { "$($_.classname)::$($_.name) - $($_.skipped.message)" }
```

A full run is valid proof only when `skipped` is 0 and `failed` is 0. A skip
whose message names `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` means the database was unset.

## Timing

Measured on this Windows host (Docker Desktop Postgres 16), serial, full
suite. Run-to-run variance is large (disk sync inside Docker Desktop), so
compare only back-to-back runs in a quiet window.

| Revision | Runs (wall) | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Before OPS-01 (in-place migrate per module, TRUNCATE per test) | 80.3 min, 41.4 min; peak 91 sessions | 2 timing-sensitive tick failures in the 80-min run; a third attempt reached 34% in 4 h while the machine slept |
| Serial template clone + DELETE cleanup (OPS-01 phase 1) | 30.8 min (back-to-back with the 41.4-min baseline); peak 22 sessions | template clone without DELETE cleanup: 41.4 min, one 23-min disk stall in a tick test |
| `-n 2 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, normal command) | 7.7 min, 8.4 min; peak 35 and 32 sessions | 2128 passed, 0 skipped, 0 failed both times; node IDs identical to serial collection |
| `-n 3 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, one run) | 6.5 min; peak 41 sessions | 2128 passed; not repeated, so not the default |

Expected `-n 2` full run: about 8-10 min; cap at 20 min. Expected serial full
run: about 30-45 min; cap at 90 min. Use `-n 2`, never `-n auto`: the suite is
disk-bound inside Docker Desktop, and each worker adds about 10-20 sessions
against `max_connections = 100`.

If a timing-sensitive test (S4 tick hung-stage and claims replay, health skew
and overlap race, drain 30 s deadline, work enqueue-lock poll) fails under
`-n 2`, do not retry it: record the node ID and its duration, rerun that file
serially as a separate invocation, and report both runs' JUnit counts.

"""The shared, read-derived money eligibility gate (LR-01 phase 2, `money-eligibility-1`).

Decision `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` (Q4) and `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. For one
Finding it is computed on every read from persisted lineage, never stored and never a UI-only predicate:

- the Finding's LATEST FindingRevision (episode bounds, matcher version, cited window assessments, and the
  `finding-episode-3` `series_limits` / `end_reason` it carries), and
- every latest selected UTC-day dispatch window overlapping the episode plus its six-hour bracket (the same
  bracket as the S3 query and requeue), with its S3 read state, live jobs, selected run/model and cohort limits,
  and the S4 match state of that day.

States, in precedence order:

1. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` - relevant S3/S4 work or staleness remains: a day overlapping the episode has no
   selected window (N3), a bracketed day's read is not CURRENT (stale selection, outstanding or failed job,
   model change), a live S3 job, a day not yet matched by the current matcher, or a latest FindingRevision
   that does not cite the selected window revision of every episode day or was written by an older matcher.
2. `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES` - settled, but a material unresolved generator series refusal remains: the
   latest revision carries a series limit (`SERIES_LIMIT` end or `series_limits`), or a selected window's
   generator-role cohort limit for the Finding's generator intersects the bracketed episode.
3. `ELIGIBLE` - passes this series/reassessment gate only. A current amount ALSO needs the exact-head
   `CURRENT PRICED` consequence of the latest FindingRevision and the existing source/currentness checks
   (`consequence_read`); eligibility is never an amount authorization.

Historical or pinned FindingRevisions are judged by the Finding's latest revision: their rows stay recorded or
audit-only. The S4 consequence read, the Finding bridge, the Financials API and the S5 upstream seam all consume
this one function.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol
from uuid import UUID

from assetops_backend.analytics.dispatch_v3 import ANALYTIC_ID, MODEL_VERSION
from assetops_backend.analytics.finding_episode import GENERATOR_ROLES, MATCHER_VERSION, SERIES_LIMIT

VERSION = "money-eligibility-1"
BRACKET = timedelta(hours=6)
ELIGIBLE = "ELIGIBLE"
WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES = "WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES"
PENDING_REASSESSMENT = "PENDING_REASSESSMENT"
STATES = (ELIGIBLE, WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES, PENDING_REASSESSMENT)

NOTES = {
    ELIGIBLE: "No pending reassessment and no unresolved generator-series refusal touches this finding. This "
              "gate alone is not an amount: a current cost also needs the latest consequence to be current and "
              "priced.",
    WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES: "The generator readings around this finding could not be put in a proven time "
                               "order, so the end of the episode is not known and no current cost is shown. "
                               "Earlier recorded costs stay available for the record.",
    PENDING_REASSESSMENT: "The finding is being reassessed after new evidence or clock proof, so no current cost "
                          "is shown until the reassessment settles.",
}


class DispatchWindows(Protocol):
    def window(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, day: date) -> dict[str, Any]: ...


class FindingRows(Protocol):
    def finding_detail(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, finding_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any] | None: ...

    def match_state(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str, window_start: datetime
                    ) -> dict[str, Any]: ...


def _utc(value: Any) -> datetime:
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    return datetime.fromisoformat(str(value).replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _iso(value: Any) -> str | None:
    return None if value is None else _utc(value).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def days_over(lo: datetime, hi: datetime) -> list[date]:
    """UTC days intersecting [lo, hi]."""
    out, d = [], lo.astimezone(timezone.utc).date()
    while datetime(d.year, d.month, d.day, tzinfo=timezone.utc) <= hi:
        out.append(d)
        d += timedelta(days=1)
    return out


def _start(d: date) -> datetime:
    return datetime(d.year, d.month, d.day, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def evaluate(finding_id: str, latest: dict[str, Any], asset_id: str, days: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Pure precedence over already-read rows. `latest` is the latest FindingRevision row (with `assessments`);
    each `days` item is `{"day": date, "read": DispatchReadService.window(...), "match": match_state(...)}`."""
    start, end = _utc(latest["episode_start"]), _utc(latest["episode_end"])
    lo, hi = start - BRACKET, end + BRACKET
    claim = latest.get("claim") or {}
    cited = {str(a["assessment_revision_id"]) for a in latest.get("assessments") or () if a.get("scope") == "WINDOW"}
    pending: list[str] = []
    windows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    limits: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in days:
        d, read, match = item["day"], item["read"], item.get("match") or {}
        sel = read.get("selection") or {}
        wa = read.get("window_assessment") or {}
        episode_day = _start(d) < end and start < _start(d) + timedelta(days=1)
        status = read["status"]
        jobs = [str(j["id"]) for j in read.get("jobs") or ()]
        code = f"DAY {d.isoformat()}"
        if not sel:
            if episode_day:
                pending.append(f"NO_SELECTED_WINDOW: {code} overlaps the episode and has no selected assessment")
            elif status != "UNASSESSED":
                pending.append(f"S3_{status}: {code}")
        elif status != "CURRENT":
            pending.append(f"S3_{status}: {code}")
        elif sel.get("model_version") != MODEL_VERSION:
            pending.append(f"MODEL_NOT_CURRENT: {code} selected {sel.get('model_version')}")
        if jobs:
            pending.append(f"LIVE_JOB: {code} " + ", ".join(jobs))
        if sel and not match.get("matched"):
            pending.append(f"MATCH_PENDING: {code} not yet matched by {MATCHER_VERSION}")
        if sel and episode_day and wa.get("id") is not None and str(wa["id"]) not in cited:
            pending.append(f"FINDING_NOT_ON_SELECTED_WINDOW: {code} window assessment {wa['id']} is not cited by "
                           f"the latest FindingRevision")
        for x in ((sel.get("cohort") or {}).get("limits") or ()):
            if x.get("role") in GENERATOR_ROLES and str(x.get("asset_id")) == asset_id and \
                    _utc(x["start"]) <= hi and lo <= _utc(x["end"]):
                limits.append({"kind": x["kind"], "role": x["role"], "start": x["start"], "end": x["end"],
                               "evidence_ids": [str(e) for e in x.get("evidence_ids") or ()],
                               "clock": x.get("clock"), "attestation_refs": list(x.get("attestation_refs") or ()),
                               "window_start": _iso(_start(d)),
                               "window_assessment_revision_id": None if wa.get("id") is None else str(wa["id"]),
                               "analytic_run_id": None if not sel else str(sel["run_id"]), "source": "SELECTED_WINDOW"})
        windows.append({"day": d.isoformat(), "s3_status": status, "episode_day": episode_day,
                        "analytic_run_id": None if not sel else str(sel["run_id"]),
                        "model_version": sel.get("model_version"),
                        "window_assessment_revision_id": None if wa.get("id") is None else str(wa["id"]),
                        "matched": bool(match.get("matched")), "match_pass_id": match.get("match_pass_id"),
                        "live_job_ids": jobs})
    if latest.get("matcher_version") != MATCHER_VERSION:
        pending.append(f"FINDING_MATCHER_NOT_CURRENT: latest FindingRevision was written by "
                       f"{latest.get('matcher_version')}")
    revision_limits = [{**x, "source": "FINDING_REVISION"} for x in claim.get("series_limits") or ()]
    if pending:
        state = PENDING_REASSESSMENT
    elif revision_limits or limits or claim.get("end_reason") == SERIES_LIMIT:
        state = WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES
    else:
        state = ELIGIBLE
    return {
        "version": VERSION, "state": state, "note": NOTES[state],
        "finding_id": finding_id, "latest_finding_revision_id": str(latest["id"]),
        "latest_revision_no": latest.get("revision_no"), "end_reason": claim.get("end_reason"),
        "episode": {"start": _iso(start), "end": _iso(end)}, "bracket": {"start": _iso(lo), "end": _iso(hi)},
        "pending_reasons": pending, "series_limits": revision_limits + limits, "windows": windows,
        "amount_rule": "ELIGIBLE passes the series/reassessment gate only; a current amount also needs the "
                       "exact-head CURRENT PRICED consequence of the latest FindingRevision and an open source.",
    }


def unavailable(finding_id: str, reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Fail closed: when the gate cannot be read, no current money is shown."""
    return {"version": VERSION, "state": PENDING_REASSESSMENT, "note": NOTES[PENDING_REASSESSMENT],
            "finding_id": finding_id, "latest_finding_revision_id": None, "latest_revision_no": None,
            "end_reason": None, "episode": None, "bracket": None, "pending_reasons": [reason],
            "series_limits": [], "windows": [], "amount_rule": None}


class MoneyEligibility:
    """Reads the persisted lineage for one Finding and applies `evaluate`."""

    def __init__(self, windows: DispatchWindows, findings: FindingRows, analytic_id: str = ANALYTIC_ID) -> None:
        self.windows, self.findings, self.analytic_id = windows, findings, analytic_id

    def for_finding(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, finding_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:
        detail = self.findings.finding_detail(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)
        if detail is None or not detail["revisions"]:
            return unavailable(str(finding_id), "NO_FINDING_REVISION")
        latest = detail["revisions"][-1]
        asset = str(detail["finding"].get("generator_asset_id"))
        lo, hi = _utc(latest["episode_start"]) - BRACKET, _utc(latest["episode_end"]) + BRACKET
        days = [{"day": d, "read": self.windows.window(tenant_id, site_id, d),
                 "match": self.findings.match_state(tenant_id, site_id, self.analytic_id, _start(d))}
                for d in days_over(lo, hi)]
        return evaluate(str(finding_id), latest, asset, days)

    @router.get(SITE_EVIDENCE_ROUTE)
    def get_site_evidence(site_id: str, request: Request) -> dict:
        scope = imported_scope(site_id)
        if scope is not None:
            start, end = _window_params(request)
            return from_ledger(lambda: imported_reads.site_evidence(scope, start, end))
        site = site_or_404(site_id)
        start = _instant_param(request.query_params.get("from"), "from")
        end = _instant_param(request.query_params.get("to"), "to")
        try:
            return read_model.site_evidence(site, start, end)
        except EvidenceStoreUnavailable as error:
            raise unavailable(error) from error

    @router.get(SITE_OVERVIEW_ROUTE)

    @router.get(SITE_EVIDENCE_RECORDS_ROUTE)
    def get_records(site_id: str, request: Request) -> dict:
        scope = imported_scope(site_id)
        site = None if scope is not None else site_or_404(site_id)
        params = request.query_params
        limit_text = params.get("limit", "100")
        if not limit_text.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(limit_text) <= 1000:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=422,
                detail={"code": "INVALID_LIMIT", "message": "limit is a whole number from 1 to 1000"},
            )
        if scope is not None:
            start, end = _window_params(request)
            try:
                return from_ledger(lambda: imported_reads.records(
                    scope, start=start, end=end, device=params.get("device"), signal=params.get("signal"),
                    source=params.get("source"), cursor=params.get("cursor"), limit=int(limit_text)))
            except InvalidCursor as error:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=422, detail={"code": "INVALID_CURSOR", "message": str(error)},
                ) from error
        try:
            return read_model.records(
                site.site_id,
                device_id=params.get("device"),
                signal_id=params.get("signal"),
                source_id=params.get("source"),
                window_start=_instant_param(params.get("from"), "from"),
                window_end=_instant_param(params.get("to"), "to"),

#: Supplies analytic assessments for the Overview (T028): `(site, window_start,
#: window_end) -> {"cards": {domain_id: assessment}, "findings": [...]}`. The
#: composition root passes the analytics service's reader in; this package
#: never imports an analytic. A domain it does not name keeps `NO_ASSESSMENT`.
CardAssessments = Callable[[SiteRecord, "str | None", "str | None"], dict[str, Any]]

FINDINGS_PRESENT_STATEMENT = (
    "Findings raised for this window. Each links to its assessed period; the "
    "Overview does not recompute it."
)


NO_FINDINGS_STATEMENT = (
    "The site was assessed for this window and no Finding was raised. This is not "
    "a statement that the site is operating well."
)


class OverviewReadModel:
    def __init__(self, store: EvidenceStore, card_assessments: CardAssessments | None = None) -> None:
        self._store = store
        self._card_assessments = card_assessments

    def site_overview(
        self,
        site: SiteRecord,
        window_start: str | None = None,
        window_end: str | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        evidence = self._store.load_site(site.site_id)
        if window_start is None and window_end is None:
            window_start, window_end = default_window(evidence)
            basis = "LATEST_RELEASE"
        else:
            basis = "REQUESTED"
        window = _Window(
            [r for r in evidence.records if in_window(r, window_start, window_end)],
            None
            if window_start is None or window_end is None
            else _seconds(window_start, window_end),
        )
        domains = OVERVIEW_DOMAIN_SIGNALS.get(site.site_type, ())
        analytic = (
            {"cards": {}, "findings": []}
            if self._card_assessments is None
            else self._card_assessments(site, window_start, window_end)
        )
        cards = []
        for domain, signals in domains:
            coverage = [_coverage_row(window, key) for key in signals]
            gaps = [
                {
                    "device_id": row["device_id"],
                    "signal_id": row["signal_id"],
                    "status": row["status"],
                    "reason": GAP_REASONS[row["status"]],
                }
                for row in coverage
                if row["status"] != "REPORTED"
            ]
            cards.append(
                {
                    "domain_id": domain,
                    "label": domain,
                    "assessment": dict(analytic["cards"].get(domain, NO_ASSESSMENT)),
                    "metrics": DOMAIN_METRICS[domain](window),
                    "coverage": coverage,
                    "gaps": gaps,
                }
            )
        service = next((c for c in cards if c["domain_id"] == "Service"), None)
        return {
            "site_id": site.site_id,
            "site_type": site.site_type,
            "window": {
                "start": window_start,
                "end": window_end,
                "basis": basis,
                "hours": (
                    None
                    if window_start is None or window_end is None
                    else _fmt(_hours(_seconds(window_start, window_end)), 2)
                ),
            },
            "accepted_in_window": len(window.records),
            # Review F5: these are (source, gateway) pairs, and are named so.
            "source_gateway_pairs_in_window": len(
                {(r.source_id, r.gateway_id) for r in window.records}
            ),
            "objective": {
                "assessment": dict(NO_ASSESSMENT),
                "service_delivered": None if service is None else service["metrics"][0],
            },

/**
 * Read-only client for a Site's mini-grid Overview (T027 phase 2).
 *
 * The Overview read lives beside Site > Evidence (`GET
 * /api/sites/{id}/overview`, `evidence_api.py`) and, like it, is an operator
 * capability served with the Simulator Lab gate closed. Every value is the
 * backend's: card state, metrics, coverage gaps and the timeline are computed
 * from accepted evidence; nothing here derives a number.
 *
 * Values are exact decimal text, never floats. Failures are named states
 * (`EvidenceRead`), and a response of the wrong shape is `unavailable` rather
 * than a partial page.
 */

import type { TimelineSource } from "./assessmentGraphics";
import { SITES_API_PATH } from "./siteDirectoryClient";
import {
  arrayOf,
  hasKeys,
  isObject,
  query,
  read,
  type EvidenceRead,
  type EvidenceWindowQuery,
} from "./siteEvidenceClient";

export interface OverviewAssessment {
  /** `NO_ASSESSMENT` until an analytic exists; never `UNKNOWN` for that. */
  status: string;
  label: string;
  reason: string;
  /** T028: present when an analytic assessed this domain. */
  capability_id?: string;
  capability_display_name?: string;
  model_version?: string;
  assessment_ids?: string[];
  lead_assessment_id?: string;
  lead_revision?: number;
  /**
   * T028 presentation checkpoint 2a: the lead assessment's stored segments,
   * interval and fuel bounds, copied for the card's compact timeline so the
   * card draws the same period as its Finding without fetching it.
   */
  lead_timeline?: CardTimeline;
}

export interface CardTimeline extends TimelineSource {
  segments: (TimelineSource["segments"][number] & { outcome: string })[];
  fuel: {
    status: string;
    low_litres: string | null;
    high_litres: string | null;
    label: string;
    text: string | null;
    note: string;
  };
}

export interface OverviewEvidenceLink {
  device_id: string;
  signal_id: string;
  message_id: string;
}

export interface OverviewMetric {
  metric_id: string;
  label: string;
  unit: string;
  value: string | null;
  at: string | null;
  basis: string;
  /** Interval metrics: the measured share of the window, e.g. "2.67 h of the 8.00 h window measured". */
  window_note: string | null;
  evidence: OverviewEvidenceLink[];
  unavailable_because: string | null;
}

export interface OverviewCoverage {
  device_id: string;
  signal_id: string;
  status:
    | "REPORTED"
    | "NOT_REPORTED"
    | "NO_GOOD_RECORD"
    | "NO_INTERVAL_SPAN"
    | "OVERLAPPING_ONLY"
    | "OVERLAPPING_OR_NO_SPAN"
    | string;
  records: number;
  good: number;
  /** Records a metric can read: non-overlapping, with an interval span where needed. */
  usable: number;
  unavailable: number;
  last_source_time: string | null;
}

export interface SiteOverviewReadModel {
  site_id: string;
  site_type: string;
  window: {
    start: string | null;
    end: string | null;
    basis: string;
    hours: string | null;
  };

  source_gateway_pairs_in_window: number;

    assessment: OverviewAssessment;
    service_delivered: OverviewMetric | null;
  };

  interval_statement: string;
  timeline: OverviewSeries[];
  timeline_statement: string;

  findings_statement: string;
}

  getOverview(
    siteId: string,

  ): Promise<EvidenceRead<SiteOverviewReadModel>>;

const METRIC_KEYS = [
  "metric_id",
  "label",
  "unit",
  "value",
  "at",
  "basis",
  "window_note",
  "evidence",
  "unavailable_because",
] as const;

const EVIDENCE_KEYS = ["device_id", "signal_id", "message_id"] as const;

function isMetric(value: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    hasKeys(value, METRIC_KEYS) &&
    isObject(value) &&
    arrayOf(value.evidence, EVIDENCE_KEYS)
  );
}

function isPoint(value: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    hasKeys(value, ["value", "message_id"]) &&
    (hasKeys(value, ["start", "end"]) || hasKeys(value, ["at"]))
  );
}

/**
 * Every field the Overview renders is checked (review F4): a response that
 * lacks one is `unavailable`, so backend drift reads as "Overview unavailable"
 * rather than as empty text on a card.
 */
function isSiteOverview(value: unknown): value is SiteOverviewReadModel {
  return (
    hasKeys(value, [
      "site_id",
      "site_type",
      "window",
      "objective",
      "cards",
      "timeline",
      "findings",
      "accepted_in_window",
      "source_gateway_pairs_in_window",
      "interval_statement",
      "timeline_statement",
      "findings_statement",
    ]) &&
    isObject(value) &&
    hasKeys(value.window, ["start", "end", "basis", "hours"]) &&
    hasKeys(value.objective, ["assessment", "service_delivered"]) &&
    isObject(value.objective) &&
    hasKeys(value.objective.assessment, ["status", "label", "reason"]) &&
    (value.objective.service_delivered === null ||
      isMetric(value.objective.service_delivered)) &&
    arrayOf(value.cards, [
      "domain_id",
      "label",
      "assessment",
      "metrics",
      "coverage",
      "gaps",
    ]) &&
    (value.cards as unknown[]).every(
      (card) =>
        isObject(card) &&
        hasKeys(card.assessment, ["status", "label", "reason"]) &&
        Array.isArray(card.metrics) &&
        card.metrics.every(isMetric) &&
        arrayOf(card.coverage, [
          "device_id",
          "signal_id",
          "status",
          "records",
          "good",
          "unavailable",
        ]) &&
        arrayOf(card.gaps, ["device_id", "signal_id", "status", "reason"]),
    ) &&
    arrayOf(value.timeline, [
      "series_id",
      "label",
      "unit",
      "kind",
      "basis",
      "points",
    ]) &&
    (value.timeline as unknown[]).every(
      (series) =>
        isObject(series) &&
        Array.isArray(series.points) &&
        series.points.every(isPoint),
    ) &&
    Array.isArray(value.findings)
  );
}

export function createSiteOverviewClient(
  basePath: string = SITES_API_PATH,
): SiteOverviewClient {
  return {
    getOverview(siteId, window) {
      return read(
        `${basePath}/${encodeURIComponent(siteId)}/overview${query({
          from: window.from,
          to: window.to,
        })}`,
        isSiteOverview,
      );
    },
  };
}

        self.consequences, self.consequence_read = consequences, consequence_read
        self.analytic_id, self.th = analytic_id, thresholds

    def dispose(self) -> None:
        release(self.schedule, self.dispatch_read, self.findings, self.consequences, self.consequence_read)

    def _match(self, t: UUID, s: UUID, days: list[date]) -> dict[date, dict[str, Any]]:
        return {d: self.findings.match_state(t, s, self.analytic_id, _start(d)) for d in days}

    def window(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, window_start: datetime, window_end: datetime,
               environment: str = "default") -> dict[str, Any]:
        if window_start.tzinfo is None or window_end.tzinfo is None or not window_start < window_end:
            raise ValueError("The window is a non-empty half-open UTC interval with explicit offsets.")
        t, s = tenant_id, site_id
        lo, hi = window_start - BRACKET, window_end + BRACKET
        days, bracket_days = _days(window_start, window_end), _days(lo, hi)

        before = self._match(t, s, bracket_days)
        reads = {d: self.dispatch_read.window(t, s, d) for d in days}
        after = self._match(t, s, bracket_days)
        now = self.schedule.db_now()

        blockers: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        day_out, jobs = [], {}
        for d, r in reads.items():
            sel = r.get("selection") or {}
            wa = r.get("window_assessment") or {}
            status = r["status"]
            if status != "CURRENT":
                blockers.append(("S3", f"S3_{status}"))
            for j in r.get("jobs") or []:
                jobs[str(j["id"])] = j
            seq = (after.get(d) or {}).get("window_selection_seq")
            day_out.append({
                "day": d.isoformat(), "window": r.get("window"), "s3_status": status,
                "s3_status_reasons": r.get("status_reasons"), "limitations": r.get("limitations"),
                "current_basis": r.get("current_basis"), "run_id": sel.get("run_id"),
                "window_assessment_revision_id": wa.get("id"), "claim_level": wa.get("claim_level"),
                "watermark": {"selection_seq": seq, "input_sha256": sel.get("input_sha256"),

        match_out = []
        for d in bracket_days:
            a, b = after[d], before[d]
            if a.get("window_selection_seq") != b.get("window_selection_seq"):
                blockers.append(("S4", "SELECTION_MOVED_DURING_READ"))
            if a.get("window_selection_seq") is not None and not a.get("matched"):
                blockers.append(("S4", "MATCH_PENDING"))
            match_out.append({"day": d.isoformat(), **{k: a.get(k) for k in (
                "window_selection_seq", "matcher_version", "match_pass_id", "matched_through_selection_seq",
                "match_completed_at", "matched")}})

        findings_out, revision_ids = [], set()
        for f in self.findings.findings_over(t, s, lo, hi):
            revision_ids.add(str(f["revision_id"]))
            body = self.consequence_read.read(t, s, UUID(str(f["finding_id"])), UUID(str(f["revision_id"])),
                                              finding_revision_is_latest=True, source_open=True) or {}
            cur = body.get("currentness") or {}
            sel = body.get("consequence_selection") or {}
            state, reasons = cur.get("state"), list(cur.get("reasons") or [])
            stale_no_work = state == "STALE" and "NO_WORK_RECORDED" in reasons
            if state != "CURRENT":
                blockers.append(("CONSEQUENCE", "STALE_NO_WORK" if stale_no_work else f"CONSEQUENCE_{state}"))
            for w in cur.get("pending_work") or []:
                if w.get("last_error_code"):
                    errors.append({"stage": "CONSEQUENCE", "code": w["last_error_code"], "work_id": w["work_id"],

            findings_out.append({

                "lifecycle": f.get("lifecycle"), "episode_start": f.get("episode_start"),

                "head_consequence_revision_id": sel.get("head_consequence_revision_id"),
                "pending_work": cur.get("pending_work") or []})

        work_out = []
        for w in self.consequences.pending_work(t, s):
            if str(w.get("finding_revision_id")) in revision_ids or _overlaps(
                    w.get("affected_from"), w.get("affected_to"), lo, hi):
                blockers.append(("CONSEQUENCE", "WORK_OUTSTANDING"))
                work_out.append({"work_id": str(w["id"]), "cause": w["cause"], "attempts": int(w["attempts"]),

                                 "finding_revision_id": None if w.get("finding_revision_id") is None
                                 else str(w["finding_revision_id"]),
                                 "age_seconds": round((now - w["enqueued_at"]).total_seconds(), 3)})

        env = environment_health(self.schedule.tick_head(environment), now, self.th)
        head = self.schedule.site_head(t, s)
        observation = site_observation(head, now, self.th)
        if env["state"] == "STOPPED":
            blockers.append(("SCHEDULE", "SCHEDULE_STOPPED"))
        for o in observation:
            blockers.append(("SCHEDULE", o["code"]))
        if head and head.get("last_failure_code") and int(head.get("consecutive_failures") or 0) > 0:
            errors.append({"stage": head.get("last_failure_stage"), "code": head["last_failure_code"],
                           "error": head.get("last_failure_detail"), "source": "SCHEDULE"})

        eligibility = self._eligibility(blockers, errors, reads, jobs)
        return {
            "tenant_id": str(t), "site_id": str(s), "window_start": window_start, "window_end": window_end,

                         "last_error": j.get("last_error")} for j in jobs.values()],
            "match": match_out, "findings": findings_out, "consequence_work": work_out,
            "schedule": {"environment": environment, "state": env["state"], "heartbeat_at": env["heartbeat_at"],
                         "heartbeat_age_seconds": env["heartbeat_age_seconds"], "environment_alarms": env["alarms"],
                         "site_observation": observation,
                         "site_last_completed_at": None if head is None else head.get("last_completed_at")}}

    @staticmethod
    def _eligibility(blockers, errors, reads, jobs) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not blockers:
            return {"status": "CURRENT", "stage": None, "reasons": [], "errors": []}
        reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(code for _, code in blockers))
        stage = min((st for st, _ in blockers), key=_ORDER.index)
        has_result = all(r.get("selection") for r in reads.values())
        if has_result:
            status = "STALE_PROJECTION"
        elif any(r["status"] == "FAILED" for r in reads.values()):
            status = "FAILED"
        elif all(r["status"] == "UNASSESSED" for r in reads.values()) and not jobs:
            status = "UNASSESSED"
        else:
            status = "PENDING"
        return {"status": status, "stage": stage, "reasons": reasons, "errors": errors}

function signalOf(item: { device_id: string; signal_id: string }): string {
  return `${item.device_id}/${item.signal_id}`;
}

function MetricValue({
  metric,
  link,
}: {
  metric: OverviewMetric;
  link: LinkBuilder;
}) {
  if (metric.value === null) {
    return (
      <span className="overview-metric__gap">
        Not available. {metric.unavailable_because}
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span className="overview-metric">
      <span className="overview-metric__value">
        {metric.value} {metric.unit}
      </span>
      {metric.at === null ? null : (
        <span className="overview-note"> at {metric.at}</span>
      )}
      {metric.window_note === null ? null : (
        <span className="overview-metric__window">{metric.window_note}</span>
      )}
      <span className="overview-metric__basis">{metric.basis}</span>
      {metric.evidence.length === 0 ? null : (
        <span className="overview-metric__evidence">
          Evidence:{" "}
          {metric.evidence.map((item, index) => (
            <span key={item.message_id}>
              {index === 0 ? null : ", "}
              <Link
                data-overview-evidence
                to={link(signalOf(item), item.message_id)}
                aria-label={`Inspect the ${signalOf(item)} record behind ${metric.label}`}
              >
                {signalOf(item)}
              </Link>
            </span>
          ))}
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

function DomainCard({
  label,
  card,
  profile,
  link,
  findingIds,
  findingHref,
  dispatchHref,
  analysisHref,
}: {

/**
 * The mini-grid Site Overview body (T027 phase 2, AC11, AC22).
 *
 * "Is this site meeting its operating objective, and what matters now?" in the
 * v6.9 8.18 frame: the objective strip, one card per profile domain, the
 * selected-window timeline, then Findings. Everything shown is the backend
 * Overview read model's, computed from accepted evidence only; the profile
 * decides which cards render and in which order, never their state.
 *
 * No analytic exists yet, so every card and the objective strip show the
 * neutral "No assessment yet". "Unknown" is never used for that. A metric whose
 * signal has no record a metric can use in the window (none, none GOOD, no
 * interval span, or only overlapping records) shows no value and names the gap
 * with the backend's reason; the card lists the gap from the backend's
 * coverage, not from the profile. The timeline is context, not a status source. * Evidence links open the Site > Evidence record inspector at the address
 * `evidenceHref` builds (`/sites/{id}/evidence?signal=DEV/SIG&record=MSG`);
 * the shell supplies the builder, because the substrate does not know routes.

import { AwaitingEvidenceMigration } from "./AwaitingEvidenceMigration";
import { awaitingMigration } from "./siteEvidenceClient";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import { Badge, Fact, FactList, Panel } from "../ui";
import { AssessedPeriodTimeline, FuelRange } from "./assessmentGraphics";

  capabilityPresentation,
  orderedOverviewCards,
  presentationProfileFor,
  type DomainPresentationProfile,
} from "./presentationProfile";
import type { EvidenceRead } from "./siteEvidenceClient";

  OverviewCard,

export const SITE_OVERVIEW_LOADING = "Loading the site's overview.";

export interface SiteOverviewProps {
  siteId: string;
  client: SiteOverviewClient;
  /** The inspector address for one accepted record of one signal. */
  evidenceHref: (signal: string, messageId: string) => string;
  /** The Site > Evidence destination. */
  evidencePageHref: string;
  /** T028: the Finding detail address for one assessment id. */
  findingHref?: (assessmentId: string) => string;
  /** T028: Performance > Dispatch, optionally selecting one interval. */
  dispatchHref?: (assessmentId?: string) => string;
  /**
   * The Analysis of an assessed period: the Finding's Analysis tab, or
   * Dispatch's Analysis view for a Needed period without a Finding.
   */
  analysisHref?: (assessmentId: string, isFinding: boolean) => string;
}

export function SiteOverview({
  siteId,
  client,
  evidenceHref,
  evidencePageHref,
  findingHref,
  dispatchHref,
  analysisHref,
}: SiteOverviewProps) {
  const [result, setResult] =
    useState<EvidenceRead<SiteOverviewReadModel> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let current = true;
    setResult(null);
    client.getOverview(siteId, {}).then((read) => {
      if (current) {
        setResult(read);
      }
    });
    return () => {
      current = false;
    };
  }, [client, siteId]);

  if (result === null) {
    return <p>{SITE_OVERVIEW_LOADING}</p>;
  }
  if (awaitingMigration(result)) {
    return <AwaitingEvidenceMigration surface="overview" />;
  }
  if (result.status !== "loaded") {
    return (
      <Panel
        heading="Overview unavailable"
        headingId="site-overview-unavailable-heading"
      >
        <p>
          The Overview could not be read
          {result.status === "unavailable" && result.message !== null
            ? `: ${result.message}`
            : "."}{" "}
          This is a statement about the read, not about the site&apos;s
          evidence.
        </p>
      </Panel>
    );
  }

  const overview = result.value;
  const profile = presentationProfileFor(overview.site_type);
  const cards = orderedOverviewCards(profile, overview.cards);
  const link = (signal: string, messageId: string) =>
    evidenceHref(signal, messageId);
  // The Overview links to the backend's Findings; it never recomputes one.
  const findings = overview.findings as OverviewFinding[];
  const findingIds = new Set(
    findings.flatMap((f) => (f.finding_id === null ? [] : [f.finding_id])),
  );

  return (
    <div
      className="site-overview"
      data-overview-state={
        overview.window.start === null ? "empty" : "populated"
      }
    >
      <ObjectiveStrip
        overview={overview}
        evidencePageHref={evidencePageHref}
        link={link}
      />
      {cards.length === 0 ? null : (
        <section
          className="overview-cards"
          aria-labelledby="site-overview-domains-heading"
        >
          <h2
            className="overview-cards__heading"
            id="site-overview-domains-heading"
          >
            Domains
          </h2>
          <div className="overview-cards__grid">
            {cards.map(({ label, card }) => (
              <DomainCard
                key={card.domain_id}
                label={label}
                card={card}
                profile={profile}
                link={link}
                findingIds={findingIds}
                findingHref={findingHref}
                dispatchHref={dispatchHref}
                analysisHref={analysisHref}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="overview-note">{overview.interval_statement}</p>
        </section>
      )}
      <Timeline overview={overview} />
      <Panel heading="Findings" headingId="site-overview-findings-heading">
        <p>{overview.findings_statement}</p>
        {findings.length === 0 || findingHref === undefined ? null : (
              <li key={finding.assessment_id}>
                <Link
                  to={findingHref(finding.assessment_id)}
                  data-overview-finding-link={finding.assessment_id}
                >
                  {finding.reading === undefined
                    ? "Assessed generator period"
                    : `${finding.reading.outcome}: ${finding.reading.headline}`}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Panel>
    </div>
  );
}

type LinkBuilder = (signal: string, messageId: string) => string;

/** The Overview's `findings`: the backend's lineage summaries, as `/findings` lists them. */
interface OverviewFinding {
  assessment_id: string;
  finding_id: string | null;
  label: string;
  summary: string;
  /** The backend's plain reading (`plain.reading`), read time. */
  reading?: { outcome: string; headline: string };
}

  evidencePageHref,

S6-01 creates one immutable, tenant/Site-scoped Action identity from a reviewed persisted `FindingRevision` under the canonical Finding route. It pins the originating revision, selected assessment and accepted-evidence/configuration basis; a later Finding revision does not silently rewrite that origin. A Finding may have several Actions, but each accepted recommendation creates or idempotently returns one Action for its server-defined command key. The recommendation is a bounded proposed reserve-SOC policy trial with explicit purpose, limitation and source Finding; it is not a measured saving or verified outcome. The Action detail root is `/sites/{site}/actions/{action_id}` with stable child routes and exact `event_id` selection, and Site/Finding back-links. S6-01 may navigate from a known Finding or Action ID before the separate S6-03 Site ActionIndex exists. Hidden unsupported tabs must not masquerade as empty delivered views.

Action state is derived from an append-only, server-ordered `ActionEvent` ledger. Record stable event ID, Action and Site, event type, `occurred_at`, server `recorded_at`, idempotency key, actor kind/ID, source channel, authorization basis and triggering command/job or content revision. Idempotent identical retries return the event; conflicting reuse and cross-Site references fail. Historical reads select a recorded cutoff and then `(recorded_at,id)` order; a backdated `occurred_at` is displayed as such but never rewrites the audit order. `PROPOSED`/`ACCEPTED`, application `PENDING`/`APPLIED`/`FAILED`, and later verification status are separate dimensions. Acceptance does not assert application; an applied change or completed work does not assert benefit. A failed application preserves its attempt, error and retry relation without an effective-policy claim. No human author, approval or attestation is inferred from a gateway credential, client field, local OS user or SYSTEM fixture. Under the existing `A-actor-identity` rule, human writes need a verified operator principal; otherwise only a clearly labelled, server-authorized SYSTEM demonstration command with stable service/job provenance is allowed, or the write stays unavailable.

The supported S6-01 change is a controlled reserve-SOC edit through S1 Foundation publish, producing a new immutable effective `FoundationRevision` and an immutable change artifact that links Action, accepted recommendation, authorized command, old/new revision IDs, exact field/value/unit, validity and recorded time, and the application event. Validate the expected old revision and Site scope under the publish lock; reject stale, unauthorized or invalid edits and record a failed attempt without claiming the new setting took effect. `APPLIED` requires confirmation of the persisted new effective revision; an ActionEvent alone is insufficient. The old basis and every event/change revision remain addressable after reload. Do not mutate a Lab-only override or rewrite accepted evidence. S6-01's Execution/History and applied `changes:1#a` show policy activity with event and configuration provenance, including pending/failed cases; they do not show an effect verdict.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort`. Accept LR-01 Q1-Q4 as corrected by Follow-up M1 in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md`. Source-time order requires a versioned, scoped clock attestation from an identified authority covering each reading with maximum UTC error no greater than 60 seconds; simulated proof stays separate from real-source proof. Order an attested channel by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) +/- the attested error, not by the full measurement interval widened by error. Measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap; genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remains `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous. Where placement-uncertainty intervals are disjoint, gateway admission sequence disagreement is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`; sequence still resolves identity and duplicates. ## Outcome and authority

An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, conditionally runs S4 `match-findings` for selected tenant/Sites with a new matcher fence or version, then runs the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.

Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-14-s402d-schedule-storage-and-install-gate` (`.agent/architect-S4-02d-answers.md`). This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.

Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute per-Site consequence-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 globally leased jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.

## Schedule, state and S5 contract

1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** One timer per environment invokes a server-side tick, initially every minute. An active Site is a persisted, tenant-scoped imported Site with registered evidence or explicit schedule enrolment, whose lifecycle is neither `DECOMMISSIONED` nor `ARCHIVED`; `PLANNED` qualifies (including KOBO-001). A Site with live S3 or pending consequence work remains selected until that work is resolved, even if its enrolment changes. Report excluded Sites that still have work; never silently discard it. Enumerate by stable IDs from the persisted Site registry on every tick; â€œnewly activatedâ€ in proof means newly imported/enrolled, not a lifecycle transition. Bound Sites, Site-scoped S3 leases, and consequence items per tick with a durable fair cursor. Extend the assessment store's currently global `lease_job` to filter by Site inside the existing assessment writer; its global FIFO cannot provide Site fairness. Run `reconcile_all()` each tick and reconcile every selected Site, including never-assessed Sites; measure its global unbounded cost, without silently omitting it. For each selected Site run S3, then run S4 only when there is no `finding_match_pass` for the current `MATCHER_VERSION`, or the Site's matcher fence (`_fence`: selection seq, job count/marker, revision count) differs from the fence stored in `schedule_site_head` at the last completed pass. Otherwise record `S4 skipped: unchanged fence` in that mutable head and append no pass row. Due, leased or FAILED S3 work does not by itself suppress a match when the fence moves. The matcher's CURRENT-window rule leaves those windows' episodes unchanged; unrelated current windows can advance after a new selection moves the fence. Drain consequence work after Finding commits. A bounded tick may leave work for later ticks.
2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use database claims for the environment tick and Site stage sequence across hosts; local timer overlap prevention is insufficient. Never overlap S3 leasing/processing and S4 matching on the same Site, including a second host. The matcher fence includes Site recompute-job statuses; report `FindingFenceMoved` as retryable typed S4 `FENCE_MOVED`, not a generic success. Preserve S3's five-minute lease and retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. An expired/crashed claim resumes without duplicate output. Bound tick and Site wall time with server-side `statement_timeout` and `lock_timeout`, plus a killable child process per stage for a Python hang. The Site S3 stage bound must be shorter than the five-minute lease; after a killed job, expose its lease expiry and retry, never assert immediate completion. Both DB stall and nonreturning-stage injections must yield typed `TIMEOUT` and retryable work. Give schedule-level discovery/stage failures retry/backoff without advancing success; do not add an independent retry to S3 or consequence rows. A terminal FAILED S3 job stays live and will not be re-enqueued by `reconcile`; expose its ID and `last_error`, alarm, and provide `assess_dispatch requeue --tenant-id --site-id --start --end` through the existing `AssessmentStore.enqueue` path for an operator to re-enqueue that default window after fixing the cause. Document the command and confirm the new DEFAULT selection supersedes the old failure; `assess` creates an EXPLICIT run and is not recovery.
3. **Durable state and health.** Migration `0013` adds `schedule_tick_head` (mutable environment owner, last tick start/success/failure, heartbeat, retry due and claim age), `schedule_site_head` (mutable per-Site claim/stage, selected/processed/deferred counts, last failure and last completed matcher fence), `schedule_cursor` (mutable fair position), and `schedule_enrolment` (mutable explicit Site enrolment). Use DB claims or equivalent expiring ownership in these records; do not add unbounded tick rows by default. The ratified eighth PostgreSQL writer, `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/adapters/postgres_schedule_store.py`, is the sole runtime writer of these four tables. Amend `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1` rule 8 for this exact adapter path and these tables only: add writer allowlist and non-vacuity checks for an observed SQL write and driver import; allow `INSERT` and name the allowed mutable schedule-state `UPDATE` columns in the guard. Preserve reverse ownership: no other runtime module writes these tables, and this adapter writes no other product table. Test rejection of unlisted columns/tables. No wildcard SQL or driver exception, `DELETE`, `TRUNCATE`, runtime DDL, or weakening of the seven existing writers is allowed. Register a schedule domain composition in adapter-isolation checks if it imports this adapter; callers receive its port. Migration DDL is separate from runtime writing; any proposed trigger needs separate review. Claims and deadlines use DB time. Keep durable heads bounded; if attempt history is added, set a retention policy while preserving the latest failure and aggregate counts. Derive S3 backlog from live jobs, S4 backlog from existing `PostgresFindingStore.match_state` / `finding_match_pass` versus `analytic_run_selection.seq`, and consequence backlog from `ConsequenceStore.pending_work` with attempt count/age. Also count pre-0012 latest consequence heads whose read says `STALE` plus `NO_WORK_RECORDED` as distinct `STALE_NO_WORK` entries with Finding/revision IDs. This slice reports those discrepancies and blocks CURRENT; it does not backfill them. A read-only operator report gives each Site and aggregate `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` or healthy idle. Only a completed zero-work tick advances heartbeat; claimed/skipped, failed and timed-out ticks do not. Repeated Site-claim skips, stale in-flight claims, old failures and growing uncapped consequence attempts trigger alarms and documented recovery.
   The rule 8 update scope is environment tick claim/lease, start/success/failure, heartbeat and retry due in `schedule_tick_head`; Site claim/lease, stage, selected/processed/deferred counts, last failure and completed matcher fence/skip state in `schedule_site_head`; fair position in `schedule_cursor`; and explicit enrolment state in `schedule_enrolment`. Name actual columns under those categories in the guard. S3 jobs and selections, Findings, accepted evidence, Foundation Sites, financial assumptions, and consequence work/revisions/attempts stay under their existing writers.
4. **S5-01 consumption.** Add a backend Python scoped read/port taking `(tenant_id, site_id, [window_start, window_end))`. Return the S3 `UNASSESSED|PENDING|CURRENT|STALE_PROJECTION|FAILED` read status and limits, accepted input/source basis, relevant live S3 job IDs/status/errors, selected `analytic_run_selection.seq` and run/revision IDs, `match_state` pass ID/matched-through seq and matched Finding revision IDs, consequence head/work IDs, status/attempts/age (including `STALE_NO_WORK`), schedule state/heartbeat, DB `clock_timestamp()`, and a derived upstream eligibility status with stage/reason. The accepted-source watermark is the selected S3 input basis plus its `analytic_run_selection.seq`; do not use disposition timestamps or a later heartbeat as a commit-order fence (`D-2026-10-11-s3-phase-2-selection-and-read`). Acceptance transactionally creates/widens a job. For each window and its six-hour continuity bracket, CURRENT eligibility requires no intersecting live S3 job, current S3 basis, `match_state.matched = true`, no relevant outstanding or stale-without-work consequence, and a healthy observed schedule; a stopped/unobserved schedule blocks it even with empty queues. Use DB time for heartbeat/STOPPED threshold and avoid app-clock assumptions. First build with work is `PENDING`; an old result with advanced basis/work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; failed with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, preserving typed stage/error. The existing S3 read status, matcher pass and pending-work derivations are authority; S5-01 consumes this seam and applies its separate F1 money rule and same-basis `CURRENT/PRICED` test. Historical IDs remain readable.

**Per-environment ownership and timer transition (phase 2).** Installation or enablement of a persistent schedule on any owner-visible host, including the demo host, is gated on `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` landing. Private development and isolated walkthrough ticks may run before then. On installing this tick, retire the S4-02c one-timer-per-Site consequence schedule in `backend/README-db.md`; keep its CLI for one-off operator recovery. A simultaneous legacy timer would hold the Site claim and produce false skip alarms. Document exact identity, credential, cadence, bounds, stop threshold and restart commands in that runbook.

| Environment | Tick owner and identity | Cadence / gate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Local development | Developer's local scheduler; local database credential | Optional one-minute tick on a private DB; remove the old per-Site timer when enabled. |
| Demo host | Deployment operator; dedicated schedule credential scoped to that database | One minute only after `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` lands; before then do not install. |
| Isolated walkthrough database | Walkthrough runner with isolated DB credential | Explicit tick invocations for repeatable proof; no persistent timer. |
| Real-Site pilot | Pilot deployment operator and separately provisioned schedule credential | Not installed until `real_pilot_requires` and other pilot gates close; then one environment timer at reviewed cadence. |

Cite the S4-02c walkthrough for the separate same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` Leg A evidence; do not require that transient state from this tick-only proof. Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID and the upstream S5 seam becomes eligible only after work completes. Record real job/run/selection-seq/match-pass/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. Capture the command log and grep to prove no hand-run `process-jobs`/`match-findings`/`recompute_consequences run` in any Leg B step (W4). Record run-1 S3 `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`/`GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` status and limits where present, and assert only the seam's true upstream state; money eligibility belongs to F1. Use the existing source-ordered variant for a positive CURRENT path. |

Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. The independent review must race two overlapping ingestion chunks against an active tick and prove no unresolved deadlock, no falsely CURRENT selected window, and eventual reprocessing after a moved basis. The schedule claim does not serialise ingestion; if the proof finds an ingestion transaction or lock-order defect, carry it to the evidence/ingestion owner in a separate scoped packet and keep I4 open until verified. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.

## Risks and carries

- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
- The matcher fence aggregates all Site recompute-job statuses; its `string_agg` can grow without bound. Track it alongside global reconcile cost. Serialise S3 and S4 for a Site even across hosts.
- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
- Do not install the schedule on an owner-visible host before `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` lands: it would automatically move late evidence to the run-1 too-low consequence on the S4-02b money panel.
- S4-02c I3 remains open until DB time controls heartbeat, claim expiry, retry due, STOPPED detection, the S5 seam and the scheduled consequence runner's `now`; a heartbeat-only fix does not close it. I4 remains open until the independent two-chunk overlapping-ingestion proof passes; use live-job/selection/match comparisons and do not claim the schedule serialises ingestion.

Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.

Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.
